## Developer Instructions

To use this branch for development:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/falken10vdl/IfcOpenShell.git
cd IfcOpenShell
git checkout build-0.9.0-alpha2609301650
```

This branch contains the latest IfcOpenShell v0.9.0 branch with 553 merged community pull requests. PR authors can use this branch to test their changes and make adjustments.

## 📋 Installation Instructions

1. Download the appropriate addon file for your platform
2. In Blender, go to Edit > Preferences > Add-ons
3. Click 'Install...' and select the downloaded zip file
4. Enable the 'BonsaiPR' addon in the list
5. The addon will appear as 'BonsaiPR' in the N-panel
